L /// `ByteToMessageDecoder`s decode bytes in a stream-like fashion from `ByteBuffer` to another message type.
T 3 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift punctuation.definition.comment.swift
T 108 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift
L ///
T 3 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift punctuation.definition.comment.swift
L /// ### Purpose
T 3 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift punctuation.definition.comment.swift
T 15 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift
L ///
T 3 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift punctuation.definition.comment.swift
L /// A `ByteToMessageDecoder` provides a simplified API for handling streams of incoming data that can be broken
T 3 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift punctuation.definition.comment.swift
T 111 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift
L /// up into messages. This API boils down to two methods: `decode`, and `decodeLast`. These two methods, when
T 3 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift punctuation.definition.comment.swift
T 109 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift
L /// implemented, will be used by a `ByteToMessageHandler` paired with a `ByteToMessageDecoder` to decode the
T 3 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift punctuation.definition.comment.swift
T 108 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift
L /// incoming byte stream into a sequence of messages.
T 3 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift punctuation.definition.comment.swift
T 53 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift
L ///
T 3 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift punctuation.definition.comment.swift
L /// The reason this helper exists is to smooth away some of the boilerplate and edge case handling code that
T 3 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift punctuation.definition.comment.swift
T 108 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift
L /// is often necessary when implementing parsers in a SwiftNIO `ChannelPipeline`. A `ByteToMessageDecoder`
T 3 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift punctuation.definition.comment.swift
T 106 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift
L /// never needs to worry about how inbound bytes will be buffered, as `ByteToMessageHandler` deals with that
T 3 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift punctuation.definition.comment.swift
T 108 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift
L /// automatically. A `ByteToMessageDecoder` also never needs to worry about memory exclusivity violations
T 3 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift punctuation.definition.comment.swift
T 105 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift
L /// that can occur when re-entrant `ChannelPipeline` operations occur, as `ByteToMessageHandler` will deal with
T 3 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift punctuation.definition.comment.swift
T 111 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift
L /// those as well.
T 3 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift punctuation.definition.comment.swift
T 18 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift
L ///
T 3 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift punctuation.definition.comment.swift
L /// ### Implementing ByteToMessageDecoder
T 3 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift punctuation.definition.comment.swift
T 41 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift
L ///
T 3 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift punctuation.definition.comment.swift
L /// A type that implements `ByteToMessageDecoder` may implement two methods: decode and decodeLast. Implementations
T 3 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift punctuation.definition.comment.swift
T 115 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift
L /// must implement decode: if they do not implement decodeLast, a default implementation will be used that
T 3 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift punctuation.definition.comment.swift
T 106 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift
L /// simply calls decode.
T 3 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift punctuation.definition.comment.swift
T 24 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift
L ///
T 3 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift punctuation.definition.comment.swift
L /// `decode` is the main decoding method, and is the one that will be called most often. `decode` is invoked
T 3 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift punctuation.definition.comment.swift
T 108 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift
L /// whenever data is received by the wrapping `ByteToMessageHandler`. It is invoked with a `ByteBuffer` containing
T 3 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift punctuation.definition.comment.swift
T 114 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift
L /// all the received data (including any data previously buffered), as well as a `ChannelHandlerContext` that can be
T 3 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift punctuation.definition.comment.swift
T 116 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift
L /// used in the `decode` function.
T 3 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift punctuation.definition.comment.swift
T 34 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift
L ///
T 3 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift punctuation.definition.comment.swift
L /// `decode` is called in a loop by the `ByteToMessageHandler`. This loop continues until one of two cases occurs:
T 3 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift punctuation.definition.comment.swift
T 114 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift
L ///
T 3 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift punctuation.definition.comment.swift
L /// 1. The input `ByteBuffer` has no more readable bytes (i.e. `.readableBytes == 0`); OR
T 3 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift punctuation.definition.comment.swift
T 89 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift
L /// 2. The `decode` method returns `.needMoreData`.
T 3 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift punctuation.definition.comment.swift
T 51 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift
L ///
T 3 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift punctuation.definition.comment.swift
L /// The reason this method is invoked in a loop is to ensure that the stream-like properties of inbound data are
T 3 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift punctuation.definition.comment.swift
T 112 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift
L /// respected. It is entirely possible for `ByteToMessageDecoder` to receive either fewer bytes than a single message,
T 3 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift punctuation.definition.comment.swift
T 118 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift
L /// or multiple messages in one go. Rather than have the `ByteToMessageDecoder` handle all of the complexity of this,
T 3 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift punctuation.definition.comment.swift
T 117 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift
L /// the logic can be boiled down to a single choice: has the `ByteToMessageDecoder` been able to move the state forward
T 3 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift punctuation.definition.comment.swift
T 119 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift
L /// or not? If it has, rather than containing an internal loop it may simply return `.continue` in order to request that
T 3 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift punctuation.definition.comment.swift
T 120 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift
L /// `decode` be invoked again immediately. If it has not, it can return `.needMoreData` to ask to be left alone until more
T 3 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift punctuation.definition.comment.swift
T 122 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift
L /// data has been returned from the network.
T 3 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift punctuation.definition.comment.swift
T 44 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift
L ///
T 3 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift punctuation.definition.comment.swift
L /// Essentially, if the next parsing step could not be taken because there wasn't enough data available, return `.needMoreData`.
T 3 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift punctuation.definition.comment.swift
T 128 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift
L /// Otherwise, return `.continue`. This will allow a `ByteToMessageDecoder` implementation to ignore the awkward way data
T 3 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift punctuation.definition.comment.swift
T 121 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift
L /// arrives from the network, and to just treat it as a series of `decode` calls.
T 3 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift punctuation.definition.comment.swift
T 81 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift
L ///
T 3 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift punctuation.definition.comment.swift
L /// `decodeLast` is a cousin of `decode`. It is also called in a loop, but unlike with `decode` this loop will only ever
T 3 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift punctuation.definition.comment.swift
T 120 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift
L /// occur once: when the `ChannelHandlerContext` belonging to this `ByteToMessageDecoder` is about to become invalidated.
T 3 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift punctuation.definition.comment.swift
T 121 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift
L /// This invalidation happens in two situations: when EOF is received from the network, or when the `ByteToMessageDecoder`
T 3 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift punctuation.definition.comment.swift
T 122 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift
L /// is being removed from the `ChannelPipeline`. The distinction between these two states is captured by the value of
T 3 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift punctuation.definition.comment.swift
T 117 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift
L /// `seenEOF`.
T 3 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift punctuation.definition.comment.swift
T 14 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift
L ///
T 3 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift punctuation.definition.comment.swift
L /// In this condition, the `ByteToMessageDecoder` must now produce any final messages it can with the bytes it has
T 3 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift punctuation.definition.comment.swift
T 114 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift
L /// available. In protocols where EOF is used as a message delimiter, having `decodeLast` called with `seenEOF == true`
T 3 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift punctuation.definition.comment.swift
T 119 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift
L /// may produce further messages. In other cases, `decodeLast` may choose to deliver any buffered bytes as "leftovers",
T 3 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift punctuation.definition.comment.swift
T 119 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift
L /// either in error messages or via `channelRead`. This can occur if, for example, a protocol upgrade is occurring.
T 3 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift punctuation.definition.comment.swift
T 115 source.swift comment.line.triple-slash.documentation.swift
L ///
